  /** Returns the number of bits set in the long */
29 
  public static int pop(long x) {
30 
  /* Hacker's Delight 32 bit pop function:
31 
   * http://www.hackersdelight.org/HDcode/newCode/pop_arrayHS.cc
32 
   *
33 
  int pop(unsigned x) {
34 
     x = x - ((x >> 1) & 0x55555555);
35 
     x = (x & 0x33333333) + ((x >> 2) & 0x33333333);
36 
     x = (x + (x >> 4)) & 0x0F0F0F0F;
37 
     x = x + (x >> 8);
38 
     x = x + (x >> 16);
39 
     return x & 0x0000003F;
40 
    }
41 
  ***/
42
43 
    // 64 bit java version of the C function from above

2. pop : Replaced bitwise AND with OR → KILLED
3. pop : Replaced bitwise AND with OR → KILLED
4. pop : Replaced long addition with subtraction → KILLED
 
    x = (x & 0x3333333333333333L) + ((x >>>2 ) & 0x3333333333333333L);

2. pop : Replaced long addition with subtraction → KILLED
 
    x = x + (x >>> 8);
48 2 1. pop : Replaced Unsigned Shift Right with Shift Left → KILLED
2. pop : Replaced long addition with subtraction → KILLED
 
    x = x + (x >>> 16);
49 2 1. pop : Replaced Unsigned Shift Right with Shift Left → KILLED
2. pop : Replaced long addition with subtraction → KILLED
